I'm fine. I'm just sitting here.\n\n[Instruction]\n- Ask about exactly ONE thing. It's really just the work stuff like I said.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever found it hard to focus or felt restless during activities you really enjoy, like a hobby, spending time with family, or doing something you love?\n\nPatient: No. Like I've been saying... if it's something I actually want to do, I'm fine. I don't really lose time like that.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever noticed yourself feeling distracted, losing focus, or having difficulty staying engaged during activities that are not work-related, even if you thought you were paying attention at the time?\n\nPatient: I don't think so. I mean, if I'm doing something outside of work, I'm usually pretty engaged. I haven't really noticed any issues with that.\n\nDoctor: Do you ever find it hard to stay focused or feel restless during quiet tasks that require you to sit still and concentrate, like filling out forms, doing paperwork, or working on a project at home?\n\nPatient: Yeah, I guess so. I won't have any idea what was just said.\n\n[Instruction]\n- Ask about exactly ONE thing.
